To prevent any cyber-attack, a strict password policy is to be followed which include the following: 
1.	The password must be at least eight characters long and can have a maximum length of sixteen characters.
2.	The password must have at least one lower, one upper, one special (!#$%^&*) and one number character(s).

4.	New password cannot be the same as your old password.
5.	The password will be valid for 90 days.

૧) My Ration મોબાઇલ એપ દ્વારા લાભાર્થી ઘરે બેઠા ફેસ ઓથેન્ટીકેશન થી કરી શકે છે. 

1) Students can do face authentication at home through My Ration mobile app. [ refer attachment for all steps]



૨) ગ્રામ પંચાયત ખાતે ઇ-ગ્રામ સેન્ટરમાં VCE દ્વારા વેબબેઇઝ્ડ મારફત 

2) Through web based through VCE in e-Gram Center at Gram Panchayat

12/05/25, 10:56 am - Harshad Prajapati Sir IT Faculty: Dear Students,

We have opened registrations for Sem 4 students of IT and CE for the Cloud Foundations course offered by AWS Academy, DDU. It is for 4th semester students only.

Important : This certificate of cloud foundation course is mandatory for the termwork submission of the subject "Applied Operating System" of BTech (IT) Sem - 6. So you can earn this certification in advance.

Amazon AWS is a leading cloud services provider. AWS Academy provides technical institutions ready-to-teach cloud computing curriculum without any fees. This will help students to enhance their cloud computing skills. Interested students should fill up the enrollment form for AWS Cloud Foundation Course at:

https://forms.gle/P6QJFeyMdS6FUUZe9

Registration closes on 12th May 2025.

Course Offered: AWS Academy Cloud Foundations

Course Content: This course provides an overall understanding of cloud computing concepts, independent of specific technical roles. It provides a detailed overview of cloud concepts, AWS core services, security, architecture, pricing, and support.

Prerequisite: Networking, Linux, Basic concepts related to client-server architecture, Web Deployment, Data Storage

Mode of Learning: Online, Self paced.

Course Hours: Approximately 30 Hours

Deadline to Finish Course: Approximately within 12 weeks from date of enrollment

Course Fee: Nil

Main Benefits: (1) Students will also be able to perform hands-on experiments on AWS platform without any charge through AWS LMS. (2) The course will help students for preparation of AWS Certified Cloud Practitioner Exam.


With regards,

24/12/25, 10:39 am - Nikita Desai IT Prof. DDU: Syllabus of First sessional test LT subject :-
From theory classes: 
1.Language translation overview: Phases in language translation, overview of system software used during translation language processors, linker , loader. Types of language processors –assembler, interpreter ,compiler etc.
2. Compiler structure: Analysis-synthesis model of compilation, various phases of a compiler.
3. Lexical analysis: Interface with input, parser and symbol table, token, lexeme and patterns. Difficulties in lexical analysis. Error reporting.Implementation. Regular definition, Transition diagrams
4. Syntax analysis:4.1CFGs, ambiguity, associativity, precedence,
4.2 Top down parsing-recursive descent parsing, transformation on the grammars, predictive parsing

Practical sessions- 1,2,3,4
